Let's specify objective for full u anti-pair:
```
def _lowdim_fun(x):
   state = _STATE
   n=state['n']; m=n//2
   x=np.asarray(x)
   u=np.empty(n)
   u[:m]=x
   u[m:]=-x[::-1]  # length m
   v=1.0+u
   beta=state['beta']
   C = _conv_square(v)  # length 2n-1
   ymax = C.max()/m
   y = C/m - ymax
   w = exp(beta*y)
   J = ymax + np.log(w.sum())/beta
   state['last_C']=C? maybe not
   return float(J)
```
For jac:
```
def _lowdim_jac(x):
   # recompute
   u,v as above
   C=_conv_square(v)
   y=C/m
   ymax=y.max()
   z=beta*(y-ymax)
   p=exp(z); p/=p.sum()
   # grad_v = 2/m * correlate(p, v)
   grad_v = (2.0/m) * _corr_general(p, v) # p length L, v length n
   gx = grad_v[:m] - grad_v[m:][::-1]
   return gx
```
Need correlate function: grad_v_i=2*Σ_k p_k v_{k-i} (ordered) as before. Using FFT: N=pow2(L+n-1)=pow2(3n-2). Fv=rfft(v,N), Fp=rfft(p,N) (p length L padded). corr=irfft(Fp*conj(Fv),N)[:n]. This returns Σ_k p[k] v[k+i]? Need verify sign. Earlier grad for c weighted: grad_j=2 Σ_r p_{j+r} a_r. If p length L, a length n, corr=irfft(Fp*conj(Fa))[:n]: corr[j] = Σ_r p[j+r] a_r (no wrap) if N enough. Good. For full u same. We'll multiply 2/m.

But note p based on y=C/m. Since C from v. `y` values scale around 2. β maybe 20-100. For β large, p concentrated on max. Gradient of logsumexp. Good.

Box constraints x∈[-1,1] via L-BFGS-B `bounds=[(-1,1)]*m`. If some v near 0/2, objective derivative maybe.

After optimize, exact score = C.max()/m. We can also use `evaluate_sequence` for final. We can run β schedule: start β=5 or10, then 20,50,100,200. Each minimize from previous x. This annealing focuses.

def _next_pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _A_fun(x):
   st=_ANTI_STATE; m=st['m']; n=st['n']; beta=st['beta']
   x=np.asarray(x,float); x=np.clip(x,-1,1)
   u=np.empty(n); u[:m]=x; u[m:]=-x[::-1]; v=1.0+u
   N=st['N']; L=2*n-1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(v,N)
   C=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N).real[:L]; C=np.maximum(C,0)
   y=C/st['denom'] # denom=m
   ymax=float(y.max())
   z=beta*(y-ymax)
   zmax=float(z.max())
   w=np.exp(z-zmax)
   s=float(w.sum())
   if s<=0: J=ymax
   else: J=ymax + (zmax+np.log(s))/beta
   st['cache_key']=_x_key(x, st); st['cache_fa']=fa; st['cache_C']=C; st['cache_y']=y? not necessary

def _A_jac(x):
   st; same prepare x,v
   key=_x_key(x,st)
   if not _key_close(key, st.get('cache_key')):
       recompute fa,C
   else: fa=st['cache_fa']; C=st['cache_C']
   y=C/st['denom']
   z=beta*(y-y.max()); softmax p
   Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(Fp*np.conj(fa),N).real[:n]
   grad_v=(2.0/st['denom'])*corr
   gx=grad_v[:m] - grad_v[m:][::-1]

```

def _make_harm_basis(n, freqs):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    f=np.asarray(freqs,float)
    B=np.sin(2.0*np.pi*np.outer(x,f))  # shape n,K
    # ensure exact antisym? due floating maybe slight; we can enforce: B[n-1-i] = -B[i]? But sin should; for safety symmetrize:
    B2 = (B - B[::-1].copy()) / 2.0? Wait antisym requires B_i = -B_{n-1-i}. If we do (B - B[::-1])/2, then B_i - B_{n-1-i} over 2, and reverse = (B_{n-1-i} - B_i)/2 = -B_i. Good. This ensures.
    return B2
```

```
def _resample_sequence(a, n):
   m=a.size
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
```
Then normalize. For anti projection, use resampled a. Good.

```
def _random_antipair_x(n, rng, h=0.55, K=6):
  m=n//2
  i=np.arange(m)
  q=0
  for _ in range(K):
     r = rng.integers(1, max(2, m//4)+1)
     q += rng.normal() * (1.0/max(1,np.sqrt(r))) * np.sin(2*np.pi*r*i/m + rng.uniform(0,2π))
  x = h*np.tanh(q)
  # maybe add small high-freq random noise to break symmetry
  x += 0.05*rng.normal(0,0.2,m)
  return clip
```

Let's work through low-dim harmonic functions in detail with left basis.

`_init_harm_state(m, basis, beta)`:
- n=2m, N=next_pow2(3n-2), dim=basis.shape[1]
- hw arrays dim.
- store basis, m,n,diff.

`_H_fun(c)`:
```
st=_HARM_STATE; m=st['m']; n=st['n']
c=np.asarray(c,float)
u= np.tanh(st['basis'] @ c)  # length m
v= np.empty(n); ufull=np.empty(n); ufull[:m]=u; ufull[m:n]= -u[::-1]; v=1+ufull
fa=np.fft.rfft(v,st['N']); C=...; y=C/m; beta; J
store key(c), fa, C
```
`_H_jac(c)`:
```
prepare u,v,key; if cache valid use fa,C else compute
p softmax(y,m,beta)
Fp=rfft(p,N); corr=irfft(Fp*conj(fa),N)[:n]
grad_v=2/m*corr
gx_left=grad_v[:m] - grad_v[m:][::-1]
grad_u=gx_left*(1-u*u)
return basis.T @ grad_u
```
This is good.

Full anti `_A_fun` uses x directly, not tanh; v=1+u, u=x extension. If x at bounds ±1, v 0 or2. Objective smooth; gradient okay.

Softmax `_S_fun`:
```
st=_SIMPLEX_STATE; n=st['n']; dim=n-1
x=np.asarray; m=n-1
w=np.empty(n); w[:m]=np.clip(x,-10,10); w[-1]=0.0
wmax=w.max(); w-=wmax; w=np.where(w> -30, w, -30) maybe
e=np.exp(w); a=e/e.sum(); # if sum 0? not
fa,C; y=2*n*C; beta; J
store key, fa,C, a? jac needs only fa,C.
```
But jac gradient formula needs a and S=grad_a@a. C and fa enough? Need a to compute S in gx. We can recompute a from x (cheap) in jac. Also could store a but no need. Compute w again. Then p softmax(y), corr, grad_a=4*n*corr. S=float(grad_a @ a). gx=grad_a[:m] - a[:m]*S. Return.
If a clipped min? a positive. Good.

Cache key uses x length. If x at bounds, no clipping? We use x as passed (bounds ensure). In prepare, no need clip for jac? But use same clip? To avoid mismatch, use x as is (bounds). We can clip to [-10,10] in both for safety. Key on clipped? Use raw x? If bounds, no difference. Use raw.

Random anti x:
```
def _random_anti_x(n, rng, K=6, h=0.55):
 m=n//2; i=np.arange(m, dtype=float)
 q = np.zeros(m)
 maxf=max(2,m//8)
 for f in rng.integers(1, maxf+1, size=K):
    amp = rng.normal(0, 1.0/max(1, f))
    phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    q += amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*f*i/m + phase)
 q += 0.1*rng.normal(0,1,m)
 return np.clip(h*np.tanh(q*1.0), -1,1)
```
This gives mean square <h^2 if tanh. Good.

Maybe we should ensure mean x^2 near 0.25. Could scale q so rms x ~0.5 after tanh? We can compute and adjust:
```
x = h*np.tanh(q)
rms=np.sqrt(np.mean(x*x))
if rms>1e-6: x *= target_rms/rms; x=np.clip(x,-1,1)
```
target_rms = sqrt(0.25)=0.5 maybe. But if scaling >1, some >1 clipped. Use target 0.45. This helps. Do for random init.

def _with_minimize(fun,jac,x0,state,bounds,tag,deadline,beta_schedule):
  global _ACTIVE_STATE
  x=x0.copy()
  for beta,alloc in beta_schedule:
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now>=deadline: break
    delta=min(alloc, deadline-now)
    if delta<5: break
    state['beta']=beta
    state['stop']=False
    state['it']=0
    state['deadline']=now+delta
    _ACTIVE_STATE=state
    opts=...
    try: res=minimize(fun,x,jac=jac,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options=opts,callback=_callback)
    except Exception as exc: print; break
    if res.x is not None and finite: x=res.x.copy()
    print tag beta res.fun
  return x
```

```
def _minimize_wrapper(fun,jac,x0,state,bounds,tag,total_alloc,deadline,schedule,mem=50):
    x = np.asarray(x0,float).copy()
    rem = min(float(total_alloc), deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 10: return x
    for beta, frac in schedule:
       now=time.monotonic()
       delta = min(frac*total_alloc, deadline-now, rem)
       if delta < 10: break
       state['beta']=float(beta)
       state['deadline']=time.monotonic()+delta

       state['iter_count']=0

       except Exception as e: print; break

    return x
```

def _callback(kwargs):

       print(f"  [lbfgs] it={st['iter_count']} f={f:.6f} t={time.monotonic()-st.get('t0',time.monotonic()):.1f}")

Let's write `_fft_corr` with optional N and fa:
```
def _fft_corr_from_fa(p, fa, N, n):
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fp*np.conj(fa),N).real[:n]
   return c
```
For standalone `_fft_corr(p,a)`, compute fa.

Let's now write functions in detail.

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`_fft_square`:
```
def _fft_square(a):
    n=a.size
    if n <= 0: return np.array([])
    L=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=N).real[:L]
    b = np.where(b > 0, b, 0.0)
    return b
```
Use `np.maximum(b,0,out=b)` maybe.

`_fft_corr`:
```
def _fft_corr(p,a):
    n=a.size; L=p.size
    N=_next_pow2(n+L-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    return _fft_corr_from_fa(p,fa,N,n)
```
`_fft_corr_from_fa` as above.

`_internal_score`:
```
def _internal_score(a,b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01: return inf
    if b is None: b=_fft_square(a)
    maxb=float(b.max()) if b.size else 0
    return float(2.0*n*maxb/(s*s))
```

State key:
```
def _x_key(x, st):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    h1=float(np.dot(x, st['hw1']))
    h2=float(np.dot(x, st['hw2']))
    m=x.size
    return (h1,h2,float(x[0]),float(x[m//2]),float(x[-1]),float(np.sum(x)))
```
If m=1, ok. Two random weight vectors deterministic from dim and seed? We'll create in state.

```
def _key_close(k1,k2):
    try:
      for a,b in zip(k1,k2):
        if not np.isclose(a,b,rtol=1e-12,atol=1e-12): return False
      return True
    except: return False
```
Maybe dot differences due x same exact, key exact. If x from different array but same values, dot deterministic but floating summation order same? np.dot uses same BLAS; maybe exact enough. Good.

`_make_state_common(dim,n,m,tag?)`:
```
def _init_base_state(n,m,dim):
    hwseed = (n*1000003 + m*8191 + 17) % (2**32)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(hwseed)
    hw1=rng.standard_normal(dim)
    hw2=rng.standard_normal(dim)
    hw1 /= (np.linalg.norm(hw1)+1e-300)
    hw2 /= ...
    st={'n':n,'m':m,'dim':dim,'hw1':hw1,'hw2':hw2,'beta':10.0,'N':_next_pow2(3*n-2), 'stop':False,'it':0,'cache_fa':None,'cache_C':None,'cache_key':None}
    return st
```
For harmonic dim K. For full anti dim m. For simplex dim n-1. If n large, generating random weights time O(dim) okay.

But for np.random.default_rng in helper with seed int; okay.

`_antipair_v_from_x`:
```
def _antipair_v_from_x(x,n=None):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
       x=np.zeros(1)
    m=x.size
    if n is None: n=2*m
    v=np.empty(n,float)
    u=1? Actually v=1+u, left u=x; right u=-x[::-1]
    v[:m]=1.0+x
    v[m:]=1.0 - np.asarray(x[::-1],float)  # v =1 + (-x_rev) =1 - x_rev
    # This yields sum? For i<m, partner m+k? Right half index m+k corresponds left i=m-1-k? v[m+k]=1 - x[m-1-k]. For partner of left i (n-1-i = 2m-1-i), right index m + m-1-i; value 1 - x[i]. Pair sum = (1+x_i)+(1-x_i)=2. Good.
    return v
```
But using `np.asarray(x[::-1],float)` may copy. Fine. Need clip x to [-1,1]? For optimization inside, x within bounds; for candidate maybe clip.
```
x=np.clip(x,-1,1)
```

`antipair_score_and_v`:
```
def _antipair_score_and_v(x,n=None):
   v=_antipair_v_from_x(x,n)
   C=_fft_square(v)
   n=v.size; m=n//2
   sc=float(C.max())/float(m)
   return sc, v
```

`_project_to_antipair_x`:
```
def _project_to_antipair_x(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=int(n)
    if a.size != n: a=_resample_sequence(a,n)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0); s=a.sum(); if s>0: a=a/s
    m=n//2
    l=a[:m]
    r=a[m:][::-1]
    s_pair=l+r
    frac=np.ones(m)
    mask=s_pair>1e-300
    frac[mask]=l[mask]/s_pair[mask]
    x=np.clip(2.0*frac -1.0, -1.0,1.0)
    return x
```

`_resample_sequence` as above.

`_softmax_a`:
```
def _softmax_a(x,n):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
    if x.size != n-1: # fallback
       if x.size==n: w=x; else w=np.zeros(n)
    w=np.empty(n); w[:n-1]=np.clip(x,-15,15); w[-1]=0
    w-=w.max(); w=np.maximum(w,-30)
    e=np.exp(w); a=e/e.sum(); return a
```

`_a_to_softmax_x`:
```
def _a_to_softmax_x(a,n):
   a=_resample_sequence(a,n); a=np.maximum(a,1e-300); a/=a.sum()
   w=np.log(a)
   x=w[:n-1]-w[-1]
   return np.clip(x, -15.0, 15.0)
```

`_random_anti_x`:
```
def _random_anti_x(n,rng,K=6,h=0.6,target_rms=None):
   m=n//2; i=np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False)
   q=np.zeros(m)
   maxf=max(2,m//6)
   for _ in range(K):
      f=int(rng.integers(1, max(maxf,1)+1))
      amp=float(rng.normal(0.0,1.0)/max(1.0,float(f)))
      ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
      q += amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*f*i + ph)
   q += 0.2*float(h)*rng.normal(size=m)
   x = h * np.tanh(q)
   if target_rms is None: target_rms=min(0.5, 0.9*h)
   rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(x*x)))
   if rms>1e-12: x *= target_rms/rms
   return np.clip(x,-1,1)
```
Maybe use i coordinate 0..1 endpoint false.

Harmonic left basis:
```
def _make_left_basis(m, freqs, use_cos=True):
    i=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/max(1,m)
    cols=[]
    for f in np.asarray(freqs,int).ravel():
       if f<=0: continue
       ang=2*np.pi*float(f)*i
       cols.append(np.sin(ang))
       if use_cos: cols.append(np.cos(ang))
    if not cols: cols=np.ones((m,1))
    B=np.column_stack(cols)
    # center columns maybe; no need
    return B
```
Cos not antisymmetric left? No full antisym by construction; left cos okay. Dimension 2*numf.

Search drivers. Let's implement `_minimize_wrapper` with state option:
```
def _minimize_wrapper(fun,jac,x0,state,bounds,tag,total_alloc,deadline,schedule,print_every=100,mem=50):
    global _ACTIVE_STATE
    x=np.asarray(x0,float).ravel().copy()
    if x.size != state['dim']:
       # resize by crop/pad
       y=np.zeros(state['dim']); L=min(x.size,state['dim']); y[:L]=x[:L]; x=y
    rem = min(float(total_alloc), max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
    if rem < 10.0:
       return x
    state['t0']=time.monotonic()
    state['print_every']=int(max(20, print_every))
    for beta, frac in schedule:
       now=time.monotonic()
       delta = min(frac*float(total_alloc), deadline-now, rem)
       if delta < 10.0: break
       state['beta']=float(beta)
       state['deadline']=now+delta
       state['stop']=False
       state['iters']=0
       _ACTIVE_STATE=state
       opts={'maxiter':100000,'ftol':state.get('ftol',1e-8),'gtol':state.get('gtol',1e-7),'maxls':2000,'disp':False,'maxcor':int(state.get('maxcor',50))}
       try:
          if _HAVE_SCIPY:
             res=minimize(fun, x, jac=jac, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=opts, callback=_callback)
          else:
             return x
       except Exception as exc:
          print(f"{tag} scipy failed: {repr(exc)}")
          break
       used=time.monotonic()-now
       rem -= used
       if hasattr(res,'x'):
           nx=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
           if nx.size==x.size and np.all(np.isfinite(nx)):
              x=nx
       try:
          print(f"{tag} beta={beta:g} f={res.fun:.6f} xnorm={np.linalg.norm(x):.3f}")
       except: pass
    return x
```
Need `_HAVE_SCIPY` global. If scipy not available, return.

Callback:
```
def _callback(kwargs):
   st=_ACTIVE_STATE
   if st is None: return False
   it=st.get('iters',0)+1; st['iters']=it
   if it % st.get('print_every',100)==0:
      f=kwargs.get('f', float('nan'))
      print(f"  {st.get('tag','')} lbfgs it={it} f={f:.6f} t={time.monotonic()-st['t0']:.1f}s")
   if time.monotonic() >= st['deadline']:
      return True
   return False
```
Set `st['tag']=tag` in wrapper.

Need if function returns `nan`, minimize may fail; obj functions finite.

def _optimize_antipair(n, x0, total_alloc, deadline, tag):
    global _ANTI_STATE
    x=np.asarray(x0,float).ravel()
    if x.size != n//2: x=_project_to_antipair_x(_random_anti_x(n,np.random.default_rng(1),K=4), n)
    x=np.clip(x,-1,1)
    st=_init_base_state(n,n//2,n//2)
    st['tag']=tag; st['ftol']=1e-8; st['gtol']=1e-7
    _ANTI_STATE=st
    bounds=[(-1.0,1.0)]*(n//2)
    # beta schedule focus on score: [10,40,150,400] fractions [0.25,0.3,0.25,0.2]
    x=_minimize_wrapper(_A_fun,_A_jac,x,st,bounds,tag,total_alloc,deadline,[(10,0.25),(40,0.30),(150,0.25),(400,0.20)])
    # clear cache
    st['cache_fa']=None; st['cache_C']=None
    return x
```

Harmonic driver simpler:
```
def _optimize_harmonic(n, rng, total_alloc, deadline, tag):
   m=n//2; best_x=None; best_sc=inf
   # try two basis sets
   for try_idx, numf in enumerate([2,4]):
      if time>deadline: break
      maxf=max(2,m//8)
      freqs=rng.integers(1,maxf+1,size=numf)
      basis=_make_left_basis(m,freqs,use_cos=True)
      st=_init_base_state(n,m,basis.shape[1])
      st['tag']=tag; st['basis']=basis; st['ftol']=1e-6; st['gtol']=1e-5; st['scale']=float(m)
      _HARM_STATE=st
      bounds=[(-3.0,3.0)]*basis.shape[1]
      c0=rng.normal(0,0.5,basis.shape[1])
      alloc = min(total_alloc/(len?), deadline-now)
      c=_minimize_wrapper(_H_fun,_H_jac,c0,st,bounds,tag+str(try_idx),alloc,deadline,[(5,0.3),(30,0.4),(120,0.3)])
      u=np.tanh(st['basis']@c)
      x=np.clip(u,-1,1)
      sc,v=_antipair_score_and_v(x,n)
      if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_x=x.copy()
   return best_x,best_sc
```
Need set `_HARM_STATE` global in driver.

But `_A_fun` uses state `st['m']` from init; for harmonic, same. `_H_fun` uses `st['basis']`.

Let's code `_H_fun`, `_A_fun`, `_S_fun` after states.

`_H_fun` details:
```
def _H_fun(c):
    st=_HARM_STATE
    if st is None: return inf
    m=st['m']; n=st['n']; beta=st['beta']; N=st['N']
    c=np.asarray(c,float).ravel()
    u=np.tanh(st['basis'] @ c)
    v=np.empty(n)
    v[:m]=1.0+u
    v[m:]=1.0 - u[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(v,N)
    C=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N).real[:2*n-1]
    C=np.maximum(C,0)
    Cmax=float(C.max())
    z=(beta/m)*(C-Cmax)
    zmax=float(z.max())
    w=np.exp(z-zmax)
    s=float(w.sum())
    J=Cmax + (m/beta)*(zmax + np.log(s))
    st['cache_key']=_x_key(c,st); st['cache_fa']=fa; st['cache_C']=C
    return float(J)
```
If z range large, exp underflow but zmax 0? Actually z≤0, zmax=0 if Cmax achieved; due floating maybe -1e-12; one exp ~1. Good.

`_H_jac`:
```
def _H_jac(c):
   st=_HARM_STATE ... prepare c,u,v
   key=_x_key(c,st)
   if st.get('cache_fa') is not None and _key_close(st['cache_key'],key):
       fa=st['cache_fa']; C=st['cache_C']
   else:
       fa=np.fft.rfft(v,N); C=...
   Cmax=...
   z=(beta/m)*(C-Cmax); zmax...; p=exp(z-zmax); p/=sum
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(fp*np.conj(fa),N).real[:n]
   gv=2.0*corr
   gx=gv[:m]-gv[m:][::-1]
   gu=gx*(1.0-u*u)
   return np.asarray(st['basis'].T @ gu, dtype=float)
```
No /m because objective scaled. Good.

`_A_fun` similar but u=x full:
```
x=np.asarray; x=np.clip(x,-1,1)
v=np.empty(n); v[:m]=1+x; v[m:]=1-x[::-1]
...
st['cache_key']=_x_key(x,st)
```
`_A_jac`: same but no tanh; grad_v=2corr; gx=gv[:m]-gv[m:][::-1]. Return gx.

`_S_fun`:

wmax=w.max(); w-=wmax; w=np.maximum(w,-30)
e=np.exp(w); a=e/e.sum();
fa=C...

```
`_S_jac`:
```
# reconstruct a with same clip
...
if cache: fa,C else compute
y=2n*C; softmax p
corr=...
ga=4.0*n*corr
S=float(np.dot(ga,a))
gx=ga[:n-1] - a[:n-1]*S
return gx
```
Note: Since objective J=ymax +(1/beta)logsum, derivative wrt C = p? Let's verify: J=(1/beta)logΣe^{β y}. dJ/dC=p*beta*d y/dC / beta =p* d y/dC = p*2n. Then dJ/d(a_i)=2n*∂(Σp C)/da_i=2n*2corr=4n corr. Good.

If using stable z with yymax, p formula. Good.

Now, one subtle point: In full simplex objective, y=2n C, sum a=1. But C from softmax maybe not exactly normalized due numerical; we can `a /= a.sum()` after softmax. Then C computed with normalized. Good.

Now, `res.fun` for anti/harmonic is soft C (O(n)). Print may be thousands. Fine.

Potential performance: `_H_fun` does `st['basis'] @ c`, `basis.T @ gu`. K small. Good.

Now, what if `scipy.optimize.minimize` with L-BFGS-B and callback that returns True stops but `res` success? It returns `stop` maybe. We just use res.x if finite. Good.

Potential problem: L-BFGS-B may call `fun` and `jac` with x being a view; our key uses dot. In cache, if `fun` called during line search, it updates cache to line search point. Then `jac` at previous major point? Wait SciPy L-BFGS-B implementation: At each iteration, it has function value and gradient at current point from previous major iteration. For new point after update, it evaluates function and gradient at current x (the major iteration starting point). Then line search evaluates function at trial points, possibly gradient? L-BFGS-B line search typically only calls function, not gradient. So the cache after line search endpoint may not correspond to current x for next jac? At start of next major iteration, current x is line search endpoint. The algorithm may already have function value from line search endpoint but not gradient; it will call fun(current_x) then jac(current_x). The last fun call may be line search endpoint, cache matches. Good. If line search ended without a final fun? It likely has. Even if not, fun will be called. So cache key matching should work.

But during beta change, we call minimize with same x but new beta. The first fun call after beta change updates cache with same x; okay.

Cache memory: `_A_STATE` cache key for x length; if key mismatch due slight differences (e.g., x from res.x and local x), recompute. Good.

def _mirror_refine(a, time_alloc, rng, beta=50, label='mirror'):
  a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a=np.maximum(a,1e-300); a/=a.sum(); n=a.size; L=2n-1; N=next_pow2(3n-2)
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_internal_score(a)
  eta=0.05
  for it in range(... until time):
     # weights p softmax(beta * 2n*C)
     fa=rfft(a,N); C=irfft(fa*fa)[:L]
     y=2n*C; p=softmax(beta*(y-ymax))
     corr=irfft(rfft(p,N)*conj(fa))[:n]
     g=4n*corr
     # normalize gradient by max abs
     gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
     if gmax>1e-12: g/=gmax
     # update a_new = a*exp(-eta*g); clip; renormalize
     a_new=a*np.exp(-eta*g); a_new=np.maximum(a_new,1e-300); a_new/=sum
     sc=_internal_score(a_new)
     if sc<best_sc: best=a_new.copy(); best_sc=sc; eta=min(eta*1.1,0.5)
     else: eta=max(eta*0.8,0.01)
  return best,best_sc
```

def _mirror_descent_refine(a, total_alloc, deadline, rng, beta=50.0, eta0=0.05, tag='mirror'):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); a=np.maximum(a,1e-300); a/=a.sum(); n=a.size; N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_internal_score(a)
    eta=eta0; no=0
    while time.lonotonic()<deadline and total_alloc? ...:
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       C=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N).real[:2n-1]; C=max(0)
       y=2*n*C; ymax=y.max(); z=beta*(y-ymax); p=softmax
       fp=rfft(p,N); corr=irfft(fp*conj(fa))[:n]
       g=4*n*corr
       g -= np.mean(g)? For exponentiated gradient, constant offset affects only scale (normalizes), not important. We can not center.
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(g)); if small break; g/=gmax
       # line search over eta candidates? Use accept if score < current or improved best.
       a_new=a*np.exp(-eta*g)
       a_new=np.maximum(a_new,1e-300); a_new/=a_new.sum()
       sc=_internal_score(a_new)
       if sc < best_score: best...; eta=min(eta*1.1,0.5); no=0
       else: no+=1; eta=max(eta*0.7,0.005); if no>500: perturb a*=exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n)); ...
    return best_a,best_score

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2).

The code uses:
- an anti-palindromic ansatz a_i + a_{n-1-i} = 2/n,
  optimized with quasi-Newton L-BFGS-B on the half-vector,
- low-dimensional harmonic initializers,
- a full-simplex quotient/logit parameterization, also optimized with L-BFGS-B,
- a final multiplicative-weights refinement on the best full vector.

The previous best sequence (if available as height_sequence_1) is used as a warm start.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False


_ACTIVE_STATE = None
_ANTI_STATE = None
_SIMPLEX_STATE = None
_HARM_STATE = None


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:L]
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    return b


def _fft_corr_from_fa(p, fa, N, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conj(fa), n=N).real[:n]
    return c


def _fft_corr(p, a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(p.size)
    N = _next_pow2(n + L - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    return _fft_corr_from_fa(p, fa, N, n)


def _internal_score(a, b=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    if b is None:
        b = _fft_square(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mb = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mb / (s * s))


def _make_base_state(n, m, dim):
    dim = max(1, int(dim))
    m = max(1, int(m))
    seed = (int(n) * 1000003 + int(m) * 7919 + 104729 + dim) % (2**32)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    hw1 = rng.standard_normal(dim)
    hw2 = rng.standard_normal(dim)
    n1 = float(np.linalg.norm(hw1))
    n2 = float(np.linalg.norm(hw2))
    if n1 > 1e-300:
        hw1 /= n1
    if n2 > 1e-300:
        hw2 /= n2
    st = {
        "n": int(n),
        "m": int(m),
        "dim": dim,
        "hw1": hw1,
        "hw2": hw2,
        "beta": 10.0,
        "N": _next_pow2(3 * int(n) - 2),
        "tag": "lbfgs",
        "t0": time.monotonic(),
        "deadline": time.monotonic(),
        "iters": 0,
        "print_every": 100,
        "cache_key": None,
        "cache_fa": None,
        "cache_C": None,
        "ftol": 1e-8,
        "gtol": 1e-7,
        "maxcor": 50,
    }
    return st


def _x_key(x, st):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != st["dim"]:
        return None
    h1 = float(np.dot(x, st["hw1"]))
    h2 = float(np.dot(x, st["hw2"]))
    mid = x[x.size // 2]
    return (h1, h2, float(x[0]), float(mid), float(x[-1]), float(np.sum(x)))


def _key_close(k1, k2):
    if k1 is None or k2 is None:
        return False
    try:
        for a, b in zip(k1, k2):
            if not np.isclose(a, b, rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-12):
                return False
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


def _resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size <= 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]) if a.size else 0.0, dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = (np.arange(a.size, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / a.size
    x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / n
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)


def _normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def _project_to_antipair_x(a, n):
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    a = _resample_sequence(a, n)
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
    l = a[:m]
    r = a[m:][::-1]
    sp = l + r
    frac = np.full(m, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = sp > 1e-300
    frac[mask] = l[mask] / sp[mask]
    x = 2.0 * frac - 1.0
    return np.clip(x, -1.0, 1.0)


def _antipair_v_from_x(x, n=None):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.clip(x, -1.0, 1.0)
    m = int(x.size)
    if n is None:
        n = 2 * m
    n = int(n)
    v = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v[:m] = 1.0 + x
    v[m:] = 1.0 - x[::-1]
    return v


def _antipair_score_and_v(x, n):
    v = _antipair_v_from_x(x, n)
    b = _fft_square(v)
    n = int(v.size)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return float("inf"), v
    return float(b.max()) / float(m), v


def _random_antipair_x(n, rng, K=6, h=0.60):
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    t = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / max(1, m)
    q = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    maxf = max(2, m // 6)
    for _ in range(int(max(1, K))):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, maxf + 1)))
        amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) / max(1.0, float(f)))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        q += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * t + ph)
    q += 0.15 * float(h) * rng.normal(size=m)
    x = float(h) * np.tanh(q)
    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(x * x)))
    target = min(0.5, 0.9 * float(h))
    if rms > 1e-12:
        x *= target / rms
    return np.clip(x, -1.0, 1.0)


def _make_left_basis(m, freqs, use_cos=True):
    m = int(m)
    i = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / max(1, m)
    cols = []
    for f in np.asarray(freqs, dtype=np.int64).ravel():
        f = int(f)
        if f <= 0:
            continue
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * i
        cols.append(np.sin(ang))
        if use_cos:
            cols.append(np.cos(ang))
    if not cols:
        return np.ones((m, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.column_stack(cols)


def _callback(kwargs):
    st = _ACTIVE_STATE
    if st is None:
        return False
    it = int(st.get("iters", 0)) + 1
    st["iters"] = it
    pe = max(10, int(st.get("print_every", 100)))
    if it % pe == 0:
        f = kwargs.get("f", float("nan"))
        t0 = st.get("t0", time.monotonic())
        print(f"  {st.get('tag','')} lbfgs it={it} f={f:.6f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    if time.monotonic() >= st["deadline"]:
        return True
    return False


def _minimize_wrapper(fun, jac, x0, state, bounds, tag, total_alloc, deadline, schedule, print_every=0):
    global _ACTIVE_STATE
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    dim = int(state["dim"])
    if x.size != dim:
        y = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
        L = min(x.size, dim)
        if L > 0:
            y[:L] = x[:L]
        x = y

    rem = min(float(total_alloc), max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
    if rem < 10.0 or not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return x

    state["tag"] = tag
    state["t0"] = time.monotonic()
    state["deadline"] = deadline
    state["print_every"] = int(print_every) if print_every > 0 else (200 if dim < 2000 else 500)
    state["iters"] = 0

    for beta, frac in schedule:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        delta = min(float(frac) * float(total_alloc), float(deadline - now), rem)
        if delta < 10.0:
            break

        state["beta"] = float(beta)
        state["deadline"] = now + delta
        state["iters"] = 0
        _ACTIVE_STATE = state

        opts = {
            "maxiter": 20000,
            "ftol": float(state.get("ftol", 1e-8)),
            "gtol": float(state.get("gtol", 1e-7)),
            "maxls": 800,
            "disp": False,
            "maxcor": int(state.get("maxcor", 50)),
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(fun, x, jac=jac, method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, options=opts, callback=_callback)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"{tag} minimize failed: {repr(exc)}")
            break

        used = time.monotonic() - now
        rem -= max(0.0, used)

        rx = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if rx is not None:
            rx = np.asarray(rx, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if rx.size == dim and np.all(np.isfinite(rx)):
                x = rx

        print(f"{tag} beta={beta:g} f={float(getattr(res,'fun',float('nan'))):.6f} xnorm={float(np.linalg.norm(x)):.3f}")

    return x


def _H_fun(c):
    st = _HARM_STATE
    if st is None:
        return float("inf")
    m = int(st["m"])
    n = int(st["n"])
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    N = int(st["N"])
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size != st["dim"]:
        return float("inf")

    u = np.tanh(st["basis"] @ c)
    v = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v[:m] = 1.0 + u
    v[m:] = 1.0 - u[::-1]

    fa = np.fft.rfft(v, n=N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)

    Cmax = float(C.max())
    z = (beta / float(m)) * (C - Cmax)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    w = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        J = Cmax
    else:
        J = Cmax + (float(m) / beta) * (zmax + float(np.log(s)))

    st["cache_key"] = _x_key(c, st)
    st["cache_fa"] = fa
    st["cache_C"] = C
    return float(J)


def _H_jac(c):
    st = _HARM_STATE
    if st is None:
        return np.zeros(int(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(st["m"])
    n = int(st["n"])
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    N = int(st["N"])
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size != st["dim"]:
        return np.zeros(int(c.size), dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.tanh(st["basis"] @ c)
    v = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v[:m] = 1.0 + u
    v[m:] = 1.0 - u[::-1]

    key = _x_key(c, st)
    if st.get("cache_fa") is not None and st.get("cache_C") is not None and _key_close(key, st.get("cache_key")):
        fa = st["cache_fa"]
        C = st["cache_C"]
    else:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(v, n=N)
        C = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:2 * n - 1]
        np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)

    Cmax = float(C.max())
    z = (beta / float(m)) * (C - Cmax)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    p = np.exp(z - zmax)
    p /= float(p.sum())

    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conj(fa), n=N).real[:n]
    gv = 2.0 * corr
    gx = gv[:m] - gv[m:][::-1]
    gu = gx * (1.0 - u * u)
    return np.asarray(st["basis"].T @ gu, dtype=np.float64)


def _A_fun(x):
    st = _ANTI_STATE
    if st is None:
        return float("inf")
    m = int(st["m"])
    n = int(st["n"])
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    N = int(st["N"])
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != m:
        return float("inf")
    x = np.clip(x, -1.0, 1.0)

    v = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v[:m] = 1.0 + x
    v[m:] = 1.0 - x[::-1]

    fa = np.fft.rfft(v, n=N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)

    Cmax = float(C.max())
    z = (beta / float(m)) * (C - Cmax)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    w = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        J = Cmax
    else:
        J = Cmax + (float(m) / beta) * (zmax + float(np.log(s)))

    st["cache_key"] = _x_key(x, st)
    st["cache_fa"] = fa
    st["cache_C"] = C
    return float(J)


def _A_jac(x):
    st = _ANTI_STATE
    if st is None:
        return np.zeros(int(x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(st["m"])
    n = int(st["n"])
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    N = int(st["N"])
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != m:
        return np.zeros(int(x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.clip(x, -1.0, 1.0)

    v = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v[:m] = 1.0 + x
    v[m:] = 1.0 - x[::-1]

    key = _x_key(x, st)
    if st.get("cache_fa") is not None and st.get("cache_C") is not None and _key_close(key, st.get("cache_key")):
        fa = st["cache_fa"]
        C = st["cache_C"]
    else:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(v, n=N)
        C = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:2 * n - 1]
        np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)

    Cmax = float(C.max())
    z = (beta / float(m)) * (C - Cmax)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    p = np.exp(z - zmax)
    p /= float(p.sum())

    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conj(fa), n=N).real[:n]
    gv = 2.0 * corr
    return gv[:m] - gv[m:][::-1]


def _init_simplex_matrices(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = n - 1
    if x.size != m:
        y = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        L = min(x.size, m)
        if L > 0:
            y[:L] = x[:L]
        x = y
    w = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w[:m] = np.clip(x, -15.0, 15.0)
    w[-1] = 0.0
    w -= float(w.max())
    w = np.maximum(w, -30.0)
    e = np.exp(w)
    a = e / float(e.sum())
    return a, x


def _S_fun(x):
    st = _SIMPLEX_STATE
    if st is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(st["n"])
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    N = int(st["N"])
    a, xx = _init_simplex_matrices(x, n)
    if a.sum() <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)

    y = 2.0 * float(n) * C
    ymax = float(y.max())
    z = beta * (y - ymax)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    wexp = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(wexp.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        J = ymax
    else:
        J = ymax + (zmax + float(np.log(s))) / beta

    st["cache_key"] = _x_key(xx, st)
    st["cache_fa"] = fa
    st["cache_C"] = C
    return float(J)


def _S_jac(x):
    st = _SIMPLEX_STATE
    if st is None:
        return np.zeros(int(x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(st["n"])
    m = n - 1
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    N = int(st["N"])
    a, xx = _init_simplex_matrices(x, n)

    key = _x_key(xx, st)
    if st.get("cache_fa") is not None and st.get("cache_C") is not None and _key_close(key, st.get("cache_key")):
        fa = st["cache_fa"]
        C = st["cache_C"]
    else:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
        C = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:2 * n - 1]
        np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)

    y = 2.0 * float(n) * C
    ymax = float(y.max())
    z = beta * (y - ymax)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    p = np.exp(z - zmax)
    p /= float(p.sum())

    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conj(fa), n=N).real[:n]
    ga = 4.0 * float(n) * corr
    S = float(np.dot(ga, a))
    gx = (ga[:m] - S) * a[:m]
    return gx


def _a_to_softmax_x(a, n):
    a = _resample_sequence(a, n)
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
    ap = float(a.max())
    if ap <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    floor = 1e-15 * max(ap, 1e-300)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a /= float(a.sum())
    w = np.log(a)
    x = w[:n - 1] - w[-1]
    return np.clip(x, -15.0, 15.0)


def _softmax_a(x, n):
    a, _ = _init_simplex_matrices(x, n)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _optimize_harmonic(n, rng, total_alloc, deadline, tag):
    global _HARM_STATE
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0 or total_alloc < 25.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    best_x = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    attempts = [(2, 0.45), (3, 0.30), (5, 0.25)]
    used = 0.0

    for numf, frac in attempts:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 5.0:
            break
        alloc = min(float(total_alloc) * float(frac), float(deadline - now - 10.0))
        if alloc < 20.0:
            continue

        maxf = max(2, m // 6)
        freqs = rng.integers(1, max(np.int64(2), np.int64(maxf + 1)), size=numf)
        freqs = np.unique(freqs)
        if freqs.size == 0:
            freqs = np.array([1], dtype=np.int64)
        basis = _make_left_basis(m, freqs, use_cos=True)

        st = _make_base_state(n, m, basis.shape[1])
        st["basis"] = basis
        st["tag"] = f"{tag}{int(numf)}"
        st["ftol"] = 1e-6
        st["gtol"] = 1e-5
        st["maxcor"] = 20
        _HARM_STATE = st

        c0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.55, size=basis.shape[1])
        bounds = [(-3.0, 3.0)] * basis.shape[1]
        c = _minimize_wrapper(
            _H_fun, _H_jac, c0, st, bounds, st["tag"], alloc, now + alloc,
            [(5.0, 0.35), (30.0, 0.40), (120.0, 0.25)], print_every=100
        )

        u = np.tanh(st["basis"] @ np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64))
        x = np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)
        sc, _ = _antipair_score_and_v(x, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            print(f"{st['tag']} harmonic score={sc:.6f}")
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_x = x.copy()
        used += alloc

    if _HARM_STATE is not None:
        _HARM_STATE["cache_fa"] = None
        _HARM_STATE["cache_C"] = None
    return best_x, best_sc


def _optimize_antipair(n, x0, total_alloc, deadline, tag):
    global _ANTI_STATE
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != m:
        x = _random_antipair_x(n, np.random.default_rng(12345), K=4, h=0.55)
    x = np.clip(x, -1.0, 1.0)

    st = _make_base_state(n, m, m)
    st["tag"] = tag
    st["ftol"] = 1e-8
    st["gtol"] = 1e-7
    st["maxcor"] = 50
    _ANTI_STATE = st

    bounds = [(-1.0, 1.0)] * m
    x = _minimize_wrapper(
        _A_fun, _A_jac, x, st, bounds, tag, total_alloc, deadline,
        [(10.0, 0.22), (40.0, 0.28), (150.0, 0.28), (450.0, 0.22)],
        print_every=300
    )

    if _ANTI_STATE is not None:
        _ANTI_STATE["cache_fa"] = None
        _ANTI_STATE["cache_C"] = None
    return np.clip(x, -1.0, 1.0)


def _optimize_simplex(n, x0, total_alloc, deadline, tag):
    global _SIMPLEX_STATE
    n = int(n)
    m = n - 1
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != m:
        y = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        L = min(x.size, m)
        if L > 0:
            y[:L] = x[:L]
        x = y
    x = np.clip(x, -15.0, 15.0)

    st = _make_base_state(n, n, m)
    st["tag"] = tag
    st["ftol"] = 1e-9
    st["gtol"] = 1e-7
    st["maxcor"] = 60
    _SIMPLEX_STATE = st

    bounds = [(-15.0, 15.0)] * m
    x = _minimize_wrapper(
        _S_fun, _S_jac, x, st, bounds, tag, total_alloc, deadline,
        [(10.0, 0.25), (40.0, 0.35), (150.0, 0.40)],
        print_every=300
    )

    if _SIMPLEX_STATE is not None:
        _SIMPLEX_STATE["cache_fa"] = None
        _SIMPLEX_STATE["cache_C"] = None
    return x


def _mirror_descent_refine(a, total_alloc, deadline, rng, beta=80.0, tag="mirror"):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    a /= float(a.sum())
    n = int(a.size)
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _internal_score(a)
    print(f"{tag} start score={best_sc:.6f}")

    eta = 0.08
    no_improve = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if eta < 5e-4 and no_improve > 800:
            break
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
        C = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:2 * n - 1]
        np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)

        y = 2.0 * float(n) * C
        ymax = float(y.max())
        z = beta * (y - ymax)
        zmax = float(z.max())
        p = np.exp(z - zmax)
        p /= float(p.sum())

        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=N)
        corr = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conj(fa), n=N).real[:n]
        g = 4.0 * float(n) * corr
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax <= 1e-18 or not np.isfinite(gmax):
            break
        g /= gmax

        a_new = a * np.exp(-eta * g)
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-300)
        snew = float(a_new.sum())
        if snew <= 1e-300:
            eta *= 0.5
            continue
        a_new /= snew

        sc_new = _internal_score(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new):
            if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = sc_new
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                a = a_new copy_placeholder = None
                a = a_new.copy()
                eta = min(eta * 1.12, 0.8)
                no_improve = 0
                if no_improve == 0 and int(time.monotonic() - t0) % 30 == 0:
                    pass
                print(f"{tag} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s score={best_sc:.6f} eta={eta:.4f}")
            else:
                eta = max(eta * 0.78, 5e-4)
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 300:
                    a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, size=n))
                    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
                    a /= float(a.sum())
                    no_improve = 0
                    eta = 0.08
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.5, 2e-4)

    print(f"{tag} done score={best_sc:.6f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _ACTIVE_STATE, _ANTI_STATE, _SIMPLEX_STATE, _HARM_STATE

    now0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, min(float(budget_s), 2380.0))
    final_deadline = now0 + total - 35.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**32))

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    given_a = None

    try:
        gv = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if gv is not None:
            a = np.asarray(gv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if a.size > 0 and s > 0.01:
                a = a / s
                sc = _internal_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    print(f"loaded height_sequence_1 n={a.size} score={sc:.6f}")
                if 200 <= a.size <= 20000:
                    given_a = a.copy()
    except Exception as exc:
        print("failed to load given sequence:", repr(exc))

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_score = _internal_score(best_a)
        print("no given sequence, starting uniform")

    if total >= 2000:
        primary_n = 8192
    elif total >= 1200:
        primary_n = 4096
    else:
        primary_n = 2048

    if (
        best_a is not None
        and best_score < 1.62
        and best_a.size % 2 == 0
        and 2048 <= best_a.size <= 12288
    ):
        primary_n = int(best_a.size)

    primary_n = int(primary_n)
    if primary_n % 2 != 0:
        primary_n += 1

    # Harmonic anti-palindromic initializers.
    h_x = None
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 1200.0:
        h_alloc = min(220.0, max(70.0, 0.10 * (final_deadline - time.monotonic())))
        try:
            h_x, h_sc = _optimize_harmonic(primary_n, rng, h_alloc, time.monotonic() + h_alloc, f"harmonic{primary_n}")
            if h_x is not None and np.isfinite(h_sc):
                sc, v = _antipair_score_and_v(h_x, primary_n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = (v / float(v.size)).copy()
        except Exception as exc:
            print("harmonic phase failed:", repr(exc))

    # Anti-palindromic full-coordinate L-BFGS-B.
    anti_inits = []

    if best_a is not None:
        anti_inits.append(("given", _project_to_antipair_x(best_a, primary_n)))

    if h_x is not None:
        anti_inits.append(("harm", h_x))

    anti_inits.append(("rand1", _random_antipair_x(primary_n, rng, K=4, h=0.55)))
    anti_inits.append(("rand2", _random_antipair_x(primary_n, rng, K=7, h=0.72)))

    t_left = (np.arange(primary_n // 2, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(primary_n // 2)
    anti_inits.append(("sine", np.clip(0.75 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t_left), -1.0, 1.0)))

    anti_best_x = anti_inits[0][1] if anti_inits else None
    anti_best_score = best_score

    # Allocate anti-phase time, leaving room for full simplex and final refinement.
    anti_remain = max(0.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 900.0)
    if anti_remain > 120.0:
        anti_alloc_total = min(1050.0, anti_remain)
        # Stronger first pass from existing best, then diverse randoms.
        for idx, (tag, x0) in enumerate(anti_inits):
            now = time.monotonic()
            leave = 750.0 if idx == 0 else 650.0
            if now >= final_deadline - leave:
                break
            alloc = min(
                420.0 if idx == 0 else 240.0,
                anti_alloc_total / max(1, len(anti_inits)) * (1.8 if idx == 0 else 1.0),
                max(0.0, final_deadline - now - leave),
            )
            if alloc < 60.0:
                continue
            try:
                x = _optimize_antipair(primary_n, x0, alloc, time.monotonic() + alloc, f"anti{primary_n}-{tag}")
                if x is None:
                    continue
                sc, v = _antipair_score_and_v(x, primary_n)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    a = (v / float(v.size)).copy()
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    if sc < anti_best_score:
                        anti_best_score = sc
                        anti_best_x = x.copy()
                    print(f"anti{primary_n}-{tag} score={sc:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
            except Exception as exc:
                print(f"anti task {tag} failed:", repr(exc))

    # Continue anti refinement from its best half-vector if time remains.
    if anti_best_x is not None and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 650.0:
        alloc = min(260.0, max(80.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 650.0))
        try:
            x = _optimize_antipair(primary_n, anti_best_x, alloc, time.monotonic() + alloc, f"anti{primary_n}-continue")
            if x is not None:
                sc, v = _antipair_score_and_v(x, primary_n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = (v / float(v.size)).copy()
                print(f"anti{primary_n}-continue score={sc:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
        except Exception as exc:
            print("anti continue failed:", repr(exc))

    # Full simplex L-BFGS-B starts.
    simplex_inits = []
    if best_a is not None:
        simplex_inits.append(("best", _resample_sequence(best_a, primary_n)))
    if given_a is not None:
        simplex_inits.append(("given", _resample_sequence(given_a, primary_n)))

    # Run full simplex on up to two starts.
    sim_remain = max(0.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 350.0)
    if sim_remain > 200.0:
        sim_alloc_total = min(1000.0, sim_remain)
        for idx, (tag, a0) in enumerate(simplex_inits[:2]):
            now = time.monotonic()
            leave = 300.0
            if now >= final_deadline - leave:
                break
            alloc = min(
                550.0 if idx == 0 else 450.0,
                sim_alloc_total / max(1, min(2, len(simplex_inits))),
                max(0.0, final_deadline - now - leave),
            )
            if alloc < 90.0:
                continue
            try:
                x0 = _a_to_softmax_x(a0, primary_n)
                x = _optimize_simplex(primary_n, x0, alloc, time.monotonic() + alloc, f"simplex{primary_n}-{tag}")
                if x is None:
                    continue
                a = _softmax_a(x, primary_n)
                a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
                sc = _internal_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                print(f"simplex{primary_n}-{tag} score={sc:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
            except Exception as exc:
                print(f"simplex task {tag} failed:", repr(exc))

    # Optional final anti refinement from whatever best full vector we have.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 220.0:
        alloc = min(180.0, max(50.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 220.0))
        try:
            x0 = _project_to_antipair_x(best_a, primary_n)
            x = _optimize_antipair(primary_n, x0, alloc, time.monotonic() + alloc, f"anti{primary_n}-final")
            if x is not None:
                sc, v = _antipair_score_and_v(x, primary_n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = (v / float(v.size)).copy()
                print(f"anti{primary_n}-final score={sc:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
        except Exception as exc:
            print("final anti refinement failed:", repr(exc))

    # Multiplicative-weights refinement on the best full vector.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 90.0:
        alloc = min(120.0, max(35.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 45.0))
        try:
            a2, sc2 = _mirror_descent_refine(
                best_a,
                alloc,
                time.monotonic() + alloc,
                rng,
                beta=80.0,
                tag=f"mirror{primary_n}",
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = sc2
                best_a = a2.copy()
        except Exception as exc:
            print("mirror refinement failed:", repr(exc))

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_score = _internal_score(best_a)

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.nan_to_num(best_a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    else:
        best_a = best_a / s

    final_seq = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    # Optional exact check for moderate sizes.
    if best_a.size <= 12000 and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 5.0:
        try:
            exact = float(evaluate_sequence(final_seq))
            print(f"final internal={best_score:.6f} exact={exact:.6f} n={best_a.size}")
        except Exception:
            print(f"final internal={best_score:.6f} exact unavailable n={best_a.size}")
    else:
        print(f"final internal={best_score:.6f} n={best_a.size}")

    return final_seq
```
